\displaystyle+C\big(\|w_{t}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}+|\mathscr{D}_{r}\bar{U}|_{\infty}\|w\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}\big)\|\varphi\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}\leq Q(t)\|\varphi\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}},

for some
Q(t)\in L^{2}(\tau,T)
with
\tau\in(0,T)
. This, along with Lemma 1.1 on
[
39
]
*page 250, leads to
(r^{2}\rho_{0}w_{tt})\in L^{2}([\tau,T];\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}})
. Hence, we obtain from Lemma
A.10
that

\big<(r^{2}\rho_{0}w_{tt}),w_{t}\big>_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}\times\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|w_{t}\|_{L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}}}^{2}\quad\text{for any $t\in(\tau,T)$ with $\tau\in(0,T)$}.

Finally, it follows from the above and (
2.42
) that (
2.36
)–(
2.37
) hold.

This completes the proof of Proposition
2.2
.
∎

2.3.
Global Well-posedness of the Linearized Problem

In this subsection, we present the proof of Lemma
2.1
based on Propositions
2.1
–
2.2
. We divide the proof into nine steps.

Step 1. Tangential estimate
U\in C([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}})
.
First, let

\displaystyle w^{(0)}|_{t=0}=w_{0}^{(0)}:=u_{0},\qquad h^{(0)}:=A\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\gamma-1}r\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}},

\displaystyle g^{(0)}:=h^{(0)}-2\pi G\frac{r\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}}}{\bar{\eta}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}

in (
2.1
). Then
(g^{(0)},h^{(0)})\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
and
w_{0}^{(0)}\in L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}}
, and hence we can deduce from Proposition
2.1
that problem (
2.1
) admits a unique weak solution
w^{(0)}=U
such that

(2.43)

U\in C([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\cap L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),\qquad r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}).

Step 2. Tangential estimates
U\in C([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}})
and
U_{t}\in C([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})
.

First, formally applying
\partial_{t}
to the equation in (
2.1
), we arrive at

(2.44)

\displaystyle r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{tt}-2(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{g^{(1)}}{\bar{\eta}}+\Big((r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{h^{(1)}}{\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle=\Big(\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+2a_{2}\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big)_{r}-\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(2a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big),

where
g^{(1)}=g^{(1)}_{*}+g^{(1)}_{**}
,
h^{(1)}=h^{(1)}_{*}+h^{(1)}_{**}
, and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} g^{(1)}_{*}&:=2a_{2}\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\delta-1}r\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\Big(\delta D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}-(1-2\delta)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)D_{\bar{\eta}}U,\\
&\quad\ +4(a_{1}+a_{2})\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\delta-1}r\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\Big((\delta-1)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2\delta\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}},\end{aligned}

(2.45)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} h^{(1)}_{*}&:=(2a_{1}+a_{2})\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\delta-1}r\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\Big((\delta+1)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}-(2-2\delta)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)D_{\bar{\eta}}U\\
&\quad\ +2a_{2}\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\delta-1}r\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\Big(\delta D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}-(1-2\delta)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} g^{(1)}_{**}&:=-A\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\gamma-1}r\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}\Big(\frac{(2\gamma-1)\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}+(\gamma-1)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\Big)+4\pi G\frac{r\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r},\\
h^{(1)}_{**}&:=-A\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\gamma-1}r\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}\Big(\frac{(2\gamma-2)\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}+\gamma D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\Big).\end{aligned}

Clearly, a direct calculation yields the following relations:

(2.46)

\big(\frac{g^{(0)}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)_{t}=\frac{g^{(1)}_{**}}{\bar{\eta}},\qquad\big(\frac{h^{(0)}}{\bar{\eta}_{r}}\big)_{t}=\frac{h^{(1)}_{**}}{\bar{\eta}_{r}},

Next, regarding (
2.44
) as the equation of
w^{(1)}:=U_{t}
and study the problem:

(2.47)

\begin{cases}\displaystyle r^{2}\rho_{0}w^{(1)}_{t}-2(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{g^{(1)}}{\bar{\eta}}+\Big((r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{h^{(1)}}{\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)_{r}\\
\displaystyle=\Big(\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}w^{(1)}+2a_{2}\frac{w^{(1)}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big)_{r}-\bar{\eta}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(2a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}w^{(1)}+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\frac{w^{(1)}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big),\\[10.0pt]
w^{(1)}|_{t=0}=w^{(1)}_{0}=U_{t}\big|_{t=0}\qquad\text{on }I.\end{cases}

We can check that
w^{(1)}_{0}\in L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}}
and
(g^{(1)},h^{(1)})\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
due to (
2.4
)–(
2.6
) and (
2.43
). Hence, it follows from Proposition
2.1
that (
2.47
) admits a unique weak solution
w^{(1)}
satisfying

(2.48)

w^{(1)}\in C([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\cap L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),\qquad r^{2}\rho_{0}w^{(1)}_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}).

Now, we claim that
w^{(1)}=U_{t}
for
a.e.

(t,r)\in(0,T)\times I
. Set

(2.49)

\tilde{U}(t,r):=\int_{0}^{t}w^{(1)}(\tau,r)\,\mathrm{d}\tau+u_{0}(r),\qquad\ \varpi:=\tilde{U}-U.

We only need to show that
\varpi=0
for
a.e.

(t,r)\in(0,T)\times I
.

Indeed, since
U
and
w^{(1)}
are weak solutions of (
2.1
) and (
2.47
), respectively, we obtain from (
2.10
) that, for all
\varphi\in\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}
and
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\big<r^{2}\rho_{0}\mathcal{Z}^{(j)}_{t},\varphi\big>_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}\times\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}+(2a_{1}+a_{2})\big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}\mathcal{Z}^{(j)},D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\big>+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{\mathcal{Z}^{(j)}}{\bar{\eta}},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

\displaystyle\quad+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{\mathcal{Z}^{(j)}}{\bar{\eta}},D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\Big>+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}\mathcal{Z}^{(j)},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

(2.50)

\displaystyle=\Big<g^{(j)},(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{2\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>+\big<h^{(j)},(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\big>,

where
j=0,1
and
(\mathcal{Z}^{(0)},\mathcal{Z}^{(1)}):=(U,w^{(1)})
.

Next, in view of (
2.49
), we replace
\mathcal{Z}^{(1)}=w^{(1)}
in (
2.50
) by
\tilde{U}_{t}
and integrate over
[0,t]
for
t\in(0,T]
. Then based on the compatibility condition
\eqref{116}_{1}
in Appendix
C
:

\displaystyle r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{t}(0,r)

\displaystyle=(2a_{1}+a_{2})\Big(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}\big((u_{0})_{r}+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{u_{0}}{r}\big)\Big)_{r}-2a_{2}r\rho_{0}^{\delta}(u_{0})_{r}-4(a_{1}+a_{2})\rho_{0}^{\delta}u_{0}

\displaystyle+2Ar\rho_{0}^{\gamma}-A(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\gamma})_{r}-4\pi G\rho_{0}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r},

and the relations (
2.46
), we arrive at the following equality:

\displaystyle\big<r^{2}\rho_{0}\tilde{U}_{t},\varphi\big>_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}\times\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}+(2a_{1}+a_{2})\big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}\tilde{U},D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\big>+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{\tilde{U}}{\bar{\eta}},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

\displaystyle+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{\tilde{U}}{\bar{\eta}},D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\Big>+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}\tilde{U},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

\displaystyle=\Big<g^{(0)},(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{2\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>+\big<h^{(0)},(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\big>+L_{9},

where

\displaystyle L_{9}:=

\displaystyle-(2a_{1}+a_{2})\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(-(\delta+1)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+(2-2\delta)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)D_{\bar{\eta}}\varpi,D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\Big>

\displaystyle-4(a_{1}+a_{2})\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big((1-\delta)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}-2\delta\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\frac{\varpi}{\bar{\eta}},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

\displaystyle-2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(-\delta D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+(1-2\delta)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big),\frac{\varpi}{\bar{\eta}}D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi+D_{\bar{\eta}}\varpi\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>.

Subtracting (
2.50
) from the above equality leads to

(2.51)

\displaystyle\big<r^{2}\rho_{0}\varpi_{t},\varphi\big>_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}\times\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}+(2a_{1}+a_{2})\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}\varpi,D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\Big>+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{\varpi}{\bar{\eta}},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

\displaystyle+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{\varpi}{\bar{\eta}},D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\Big>+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}\varpi,\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>=L_{9}.

Then, using the fact that
\varpi\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}})
, we can let
\varphi=\varpi
in (
2.51
) and obtain from Lemma
A.5
, the Young inequality, and the similar calculations in Step 2 of the proof of Proposition
2.1
that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\varpi|_{2,r^{2}\rho_{0}}^{2}+\|\varpi\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}^{2}\leq C\big(|\mathscr{D}_{r}\bar{U}|_{\infty}+1\big)\int_{0}^{t}\|\varpi\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s.

Integrating the above over
[0,t]
, together with the strong continuity of
\varpi
at
t=0
and
\varpi|_{t=0}=0
, yields

\int_{0}^{t}\|\varpi\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{0}^{\tau}\|\varpi\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\mathrm{d}\tau.

Then the Grönwall inequality implies that
\|\varpi\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})}=0
, and hence
\varpi=0
.

Thus, (
2.48
) holds for
U_{t}
:

(2.52)

U_{t}\in C([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\cap L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),\qquad r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{tt}\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),

which, along with (
2.43
) and Lemma
A.4
, gives

(2.53)

U\in C([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}).

Step 3. Boundary condition of
U
.
Thanks to (
2.1
) and (
2.10
), we can show that
\eqref{lp}_{1}
holds for
a.e.

(t,r)\in(0,T)\times(0,1)
, which are crucial to our further analysis.

Lemma 2.4
.

For any
a\in(0,1)
and a.e.
t\in(0,T)
,

(2.54)

r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\in L^{2}(0,a),\qquad\Big(\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U+2a_{2}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big)_{r}\in L^{2}(a,1).

Furthermore, equation
\eqref{lp}_{1}
holds for a.e.
(t,r)\in(0,T)\times(0,1)
, and
U
satisfies

(2.55)

U\in H^{3}_{\mathrm{loc}},\qquad\ \rho_{0}^{\delta}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{r=1}=0\quad\text{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into four steps.

Step 1.
Note that, once
\eqref{div-Uxx}_{2}
is obtained, based on the regularity properties of
\bar{\eta}
in (
2.5
) and those of
U
in (
2.43
) and (
2.52
)–(
2.53
), we can directly deduce that each term in
\eqref{lp}_{1}
belongs to
L^{1}_{\mathrm{loc}}
, and hence
\eqref{lp}_{1}
holds for
a.e.

(t,r)\in(0,T)\times(0,1)
.

To derive
\eqref{div-Uxx}_{2}
, we see from (
2.50
), (
2.52
) that, for all
\varphi\in C_{\mathrm{c}}^{\infty}((0,1])
,

\displaystyle\Big|\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U+2a_{2}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big),\varphi_{r}\Big>\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C\big|\big<r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{t},\varphi\big>\big|+C|(g,h)|_{2}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\Big|\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>\Big|+C\Big|\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}U,\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>\Big|\leq C_{\varphi}|\varphi|_{2},

where
C_{\varphi}>0
is a constant that depends on the support of
\varphi
. This, along with the definition of the weak derivative, yields
\eqref{div-Uxx}_{2}
.

Step 2.
We prove
\eqref{equ33.41}_{1}
. Since equation
\eqref{lp}_{1}
takes the form:

(\mathfrak{A}_{1}U_{r}+\mathfrak{A}_{2}U)_{r}+\mathfrak{A}_{3}U_{r}+\mathfrak{A}_{4}U=\mathfrak{A}_{5},

with
\mathfrak{A}_{i}
(
1\leq i\leq 5
) naturally defined,
we can check that
\mathfrak{A}_{i}\in H^{3}_{\mathrm{loc}}
(
1\leq i\leq 4
) and
\mathfrak{A}_{5}\in H^{1}_{\mathrm{loc}}
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
. Hence, it follows from the classical regularity theory of elliptic equations (
cf.

[
11
]
) that
U\in H^{3}_{\mathrm{loc}}
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
.

Step 3.
To obtain
\eqref{div-Uxx}_{1}
, since
U\in H^{3}_{\mathrm{loc}}
and
U_{t}\in H^{1}_{\mathrm{loc}}
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, and
\eqref{lp}_{1}
holds for
a.e.

(t,r)\in(0,T)\times(0,1)
, we rewrite
\eqref{lp}_{1}
as

(2.56)

\displaystyle D_{\bar{\eta}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}(D_{\bar{\eta}}\log(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}))\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)+\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}U_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{A}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{\gamma}{\gamma-\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-\delta}+\frac{4\pi G}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}.

Then we can directly check that, for any
a\in(0,1)
,

\Big|\zeta_{a}D_{\bar{\eta}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{2,r^{2}}\leq C\big(|\zeta_{a}(U_{t},\mathscr{D}_{r}U)|_{2,r^{2}}+|\zeta_{a}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-\delta})_{r}|_{2,r^{2}}+|\zeta_{a}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}|_{2,r^{2}}\big)\leq C(a,T),

due to (
2.52
)–(
2.53
), the regularity of
\bar{\eta}
in (
2.5
), and the fact that
\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
. Hence, by Lemma
A.11
, we derive
\eqref{div-Uxx}_{1}
.

Step 4.
Finally, we give the proof for
\eqref{equ33.41}_{2}
. First, recall that (
2.50
) holds for
\mathcal{Z}^{(0)}=U
and for all
\varphi\in C_{\mathrm{c}}^{\infty}((0,1])
. Next, since
\rho_{0}^{\delta}(U,U_{r})\in C([0,T]\times[a,1])
for any
a\in(0,1)
in view of Lemma
A.4
and (
2.54
), multiplying
\eqref{lp}_{1}
by such
\varphi
and integrating over
I
yield

\displaystyle\big<r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{t},\varphi\big>+(2a_{1}+a_{2})\big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\big>+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}},\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

\displaystyle+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}},D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\Big>+2a_{2}\Big<\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}U,\frac{\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>

\displaystyle=(2a_{1}+a_{2})\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\varphi\Big|_{r=1}+\Big<g^{(0)},(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{2\varphi}{\bar{\eta}}\Big>+\big<h^{(0)},(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}\varphi\big>.

Hence, this, compared with (
2.50
) and together with (
2.6
), gives

\rho_{0}^{\delta}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\varphi\big|_{r=1}=0\qquad\text{for all $\varphi\in C_{\mathrm{c}}^{\infty}((0,1])$},

which implies
\eqref{equ33.41}_{2}
. This completes the proof of Lemma
2.4
.
∎

Step 4.
L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2})
-estimate of
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U
.
First, we claim

(2.57)

\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta-\gamma+1}{2}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{\infty}(a,1))\qquad\text{for $a\in(0,1)$}.

Indeed, due to
\rho_{0}^{\delta}(U,U_{r})\in C[a,1]
and (
2.55
), we can integrate
\eqref{lp}_{1}\times\bar{\eta}^{-2}
over
[r,1]
(
r\in[a,1)
) to obtain

(2.58)

\displaystyle\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{A}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma}-\frac{4\pi G}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\int_{r}^{1}\frac{\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}}{\bar{\eta}^{4}}\int_{0}^{\hat{r}}s^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}\hat{r}

\displaystyle+\frac{4a_{1}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\int_{r}^{1}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(\frac{D_{\bar{\eta}}U}{\bar{\eta}}-\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\Big)\mathrm{d}\hat{r}-\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\int_{r}^{1}\frac{\hat{r}^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\rho_{0}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}.

Then it follows from the fact that
1-r\sim\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\in H^{3}(a,1)
, (
2.6
), (
2.52
)–(
2.53
), Lemma
A.5
, and the Hölder inequality that, for all
r\in[a,1)
and
a\in(0,1)
,

(2.59)

\displaystyle\rho_{0}^{\delta}\Big|D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\Big(\rho_{0}^{\gamma}\big(1+|D_{\bar{\eta}}\Phi|_{\infty}\big)+\int_{r}^{1}\big(\rho_{0}^{\delta}|(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U)|+\rho_{0}|U_{t}|\big)\mathrm{d}\hat{r}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\Big(\rho_{0}^{\gamma}+\Big(\int_{r}^{1}\rho_{0}^{\delta}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big|\chi_{a}^{\sharp}\big(\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}|(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U)|,\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}}U_{t}\big)\big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\big(\rho_{0}^{\gamma}+\rho_{0}^{\frac{\gamma+\delta-1}{2}}\big).

Multiplying the above by
\rho_{0}^{-\frac{\gamma+\delta-1}{2}}
implies claim (
2.57
).

Next, it follows from (
2.56
) that

(2.60)

D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U=L_{10},

where
\chi^{\sharp}L_{10}
has control of the following form due to (
2.6
):

(2.61)

\displaystyle|\chi^{\sharp}L_{10}|

\displaystyle\leq C\chi^{\sharp}\frac{|(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|}{\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}}|U|+C\chi^{\sharp}\Big(\Big|\Big(\log\frac{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}{r^{2}}\Big)_{r}\Big|+\frac{|(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|}{\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}}\Big)\Big|\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle+C\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{1-\delta}|U_{t}|+C\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{1-\delta}\big|(1,D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}))\big|.

Recall from
\bar{\eta}_{t}=\bar{U}
and
\bar{\eta}(0,r)=r
that

(2.62)

|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}|_{\infty}+\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\Big(\log\frac{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}{r^{2}}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{\infty}\leq C+C\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}(\bar{U},D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U})\big|_{\infty}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T),

where we have used Lemma
A.5
and

|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}|_{\infty}\leq C\big|\chi^{\sharp}(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}\bar{U})\big|_{1}\leq C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}\bar{U},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{4}\bar{U})\big|_{2}\leq C+\frac{C}{\sqrt{t}}.

Hence, it follows from the facts that
\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
and
(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})>\frac{1}{2}>\frac{\delta}{2}
and (
2.52
), (
2.57
), and (
2.60
)–(
2.62
) that

(2.63)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}

\displaystyle\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(1+\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\frac{\delta}{2}}\big|_{2}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta-(\gamma-1)}{2}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big)\leq C(T).

Step 5. Tangential estimates
U_{t}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}})
and time-weighted estimates.
We continue to improve the regularity of
U
. First, by direct calculation,
w^{(1)}(0,r)\in\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}
and
(g^{(1)},h^{(1)})\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2})
due to (
2.4
)–(
2.6
), and (
2.43
), it still remains to show that

(2.64)

(g^{(1)}_{t},h^{(1)}_{t})\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}).

To obtain this, we see that
g^{(1)}_{t}
and
h^{(1)}_{t}
have controls of the following form due to (
2.6
):

(2.65)

\displaystyle\big|(g^{(1)}_{t},h^{(1)}_{t})\big|

\displaystyle\leq Cr\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\big(|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|^{2}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U|+|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}||\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U|+|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}||\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}|\big)

\displaystyle+Cr\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}}\big(|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|^{2}+|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}|\big).

Here, with the help of (
2.57
), we can obtain (
2.64
) by using the regularities of
(\bar{\eta},\bar{U})
, the facts that
\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
, (
2.52
)–(
2.53
), and Lemmas
2.4
and
A.4
–
A.5
, for example,

\displaystyle\,\big|r\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}\leq C\big(\big|\chi r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\big|\chi r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{4}\big|\chi r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U|_{4}

\displaystyle\quad+C\Big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{2}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta-\gamma+1}{2}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{\infty}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta(\gamma-1)}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{2}|\chi^{\sharp}U|_{\infty}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi r^{\frac{5}{4}}(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U,\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U)\big|_{2}\big|\chi r^{\frac{5}{4}}(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}_{t},\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}(\gamma-1)}(\bar{U}_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(\bar{U},D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U})\big|_{2}\leq C(T)(1+\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}),

which implies that
r\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
due to
\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})\in L^{1}(0,T)
. The remaining calculation is straightforward.

Therefore, from Proposition
2.2
, it follows that the weak solution
U_{t}
of (
2.47
) satisfies (
2.36
)–(
2.37
) with
(w,g,h)
replaced by
(U_{t},g^{(1)},h^{(1)})
and

(2.66)

\displaystyle U_{t}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),\qquad U_{tt}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}}),

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}U_{tt}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\cap L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),\quad\sqrt{t}(r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{ttt})\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}).

Moreover, we can deduce from (
2.36
) and a similar argument in Lemma
2.4
that (
2.44
) holds for
a.e.

(t,r)\in(0,T)\times(0,1)
, and for any
a\in(0,1)
and
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

(2.67)

\displaystyle r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U_{t}\in L^{2}(0,a),\qquad\Big(\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+2a_{2}\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big)_{r}\in L^{2}(a,1),

\displaystyle U_{t}\in H^{2}_{\mathrm{loc}},\qquad U\in H^{4}_{\mathrm{loc}},\qquad\rho_{0}^{\delta}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)|_{r=1}=0.

In conclusion, collecting (
2.43
), (
2.52
)–(
2.53
), and (
2.66
), we arrive at all the tangential estimates for
U
:

(2.68)

\displaystyle U\in C([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),\qquad U_{t}\in C([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\cap L^{\infty}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),

\displaystyle U_{tt}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}}),\qquad\sqrt{t}U_{tt}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\cap L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),

\displaystyle r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{tt}\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}),\qquad\sqrt{t}(r^{2}\rho_{0}U_{ttt})\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}).

Step 6. Total energy and dissipation estimates for
U
.
With the help of (
2.68
), we now can show that

(2.69)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)+t\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)\in L^{\infty}(0,T),\qquad\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)\in L^{1}(0,T),

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)+t\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)\in L^{\infty}(0,T),\qquad\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)\in L^{1}(0,T).

In fact, the regularity properties
U\in H^{4}_{\mathrm{loc}}
and
U_{t}\in H^{2}_{\mathrm{loc}}
obtained from (
2.67
) justify applying
\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}
,
\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}
, or
\partial_{t}
directly to (
2.56
). One can then rigorously bootstrap this argument to gain higher spatial regularity for
U
. We omit the somewhat tedious calculation here, and refer the reader to Lemmas
3.5
–
3.6
in §
3.2
, where the method carries over in a similar manner.

For simplicity, we only emphasize the major difference here compared with the calculations in Lemmas
3.5
–
3.6
, namely, show how to establish

(2.70)

\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{4}U\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}).

Indeed, let
\eqref{bbbb}_{1}
hold and
U
satisfy the following exterior estimates:

(2.71)

\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)\in L^{\infty}(0,T),\qquad(\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt},\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U_{t})\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}).

First, we can apply
\bar{\varrho}^{-(\gamma-1)}\bar{\eta}_{r}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}
to
\eqref{new-lp}\times\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}
and obtain

(2.72)

\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}:=(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U)_{r}+\big(\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}+2\big)\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U=\sum_{i=15}^{18}J_{i},

where
J_{i}
(
i=15,16,17,18
) is defined in (
3.59
) of §
3.2
. As can be checked,
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}J_{i}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
for
i=15,16,17,18
(see (
3.58
)–(
3.60
) in the proof of Lemma
3.6
for details), and hence
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
. Consequently, once Lemma
D.1
in Appendix
D
can be utilized, we obtain the desired conclusion (
2.70
) immediately.

However, Lemma
D.1
can not be applied directly, since we need one
a priori
assumption:

\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3(\gamma-1)+\delta}{2}}(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U)_{r}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}).

By (
2.72
) and the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
-estimates for
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}J_{i}
(
15\leq i\leq 18
), it suffices to prove

(2.73)

\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\gamma+\delta-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}).

To achieve this, we claim

(2.74)

\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}-(\gamma-1)}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}).

Indeed, using an argument similar to (
2.57
)–(
2.59
), we can obtain from Lemma
A.5
that

(2.75)

\displaystyle\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}-(\gamma-1)}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+

\displaystyle\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\big(\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}}|(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U)|,\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}}U_{t}\big)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}+2(\gamma-1)}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}+\gamma-1}(U_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t})\big|_{2}\leq C(1+\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)^{\frac{1}{2}}),

which, along with (
2.71
), leads to (
2.74
).

We continue to show (
2.73
). Multiplying (
2.56
) by
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}
and applying the
L^{2}
-norm to the resulting equality, we can deduce from
\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
, (
2.62
), (
2.71
), (
2.74
), and the regularity of
\bar{\eta}
in (
2.5
) that

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}(\rho_{0}^{1-\delta}U_{t},U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U)\big|_{2}+C|\bar{\varrho}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}}|_{\infty}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\frac{1}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}\Big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+C\Big(\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}-(\gamma-1)}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{2}+|\bar{\varrho}|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}\Big)|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})|_{\infty}\leq C(T),

that is,
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2})
. Similarly, based on this, (
2.57
), and (
2.74
), applying
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}+\gamma-1}D_{\bar{\eta}}
to (
2.56
), we can further obtain

(2.76)

\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}+\gamma-1}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}),

where the following inequality is also used:

(2.77)

\big|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})\big|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

Next, multiplying (
2.56
) by
\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}
and then applying
\bar{\varrho}^{-(\gamma-1)}\bar{\eta}_{r}D_{\bar{\eta}}
to the resulting equality, we arrive at the following type of equation:

\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}:=(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U)_{r}+\big(\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}+1\big)\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}}D^{2}_{\bar{\eta}}U=L_{11},

where
L_{11}
has control of the following form in view of (
2.6
):

\displaystyle|\chi^{\sharp}L_{11}|

\displaystyle\leq C\chi^{\sharp}\Big(|\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}|\Big(|D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}|+\frac{(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})_{r}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}U_{t}\Big)+\Big|\big(1,\frac{(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})_{r}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}\big)\Big|\big|(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U)\big|+|D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U|\Big)

\displaystyle+C\chi^{\sharp}\Big|\frac{D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}\Big|\Big|D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big|+C\big|\big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\big)_{r}\big||D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U|

\displaystyle+C\chi^{\sharp}|\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}|\big(|D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}|+\frac{1}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}|((D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})^{2},1)|+1\big).

After a direct calculation by using
\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
, (
2.57
), (
2.62
), (
2.71
), (
2.73
), (
2.77
), Lemma
A.5
, and the regularity of
\bar{\eta}
in (
2.5
), we can check that

\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}L_{11}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})\implies\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}),

which, along with (
2.76
) and the fact that
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2})
and Lemma
D.1
, leads to
\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
, so that

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big|(\zeta-\chi)\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta+\gamma-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\big|_{2}.

This, together with
\eqref{bbbb}_{1}
, yields the desired estimate (
2.73
), and thus yields
\eqref{eequ3.66}_{1}
.

Step 7. Regularity of
U
given in (
2.7
).
Now, we can show that

(2.78)

(U,\mathscr{D}_{r}U)\in C([0,T];C(\bar{I})),\qquad(\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U,U_{t})\in C((0,T];C(\bar{I})).

7.1. Regularity of
U
near the origin.
In this step, we aim to show that

(2.79)

(U,\mathscr{D}_{r}U)\in C([0,T];C(\bar{I}_{\flat})),\qquad\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U\in C((0,T];C(\bar{I}_{\flat})),

where
I_{\flat}:=[0,\frac{1}{2})
, and the regularity of
U_{t}
can be derived similarly.

First, define the 3-D representative of
U
as
\bm{U}(t,\bm{y})=U(t,r)\frac{\bm{y}}{r}
. Then using
\eqref{bbbb}_{1}
, Lemma
B.1
, and Lemma
C.1
in Appendix
C
, we have

\zeta\bm{U}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];H^{3}_{0}(\Omega))\cap L^{2}([0,T];H^{4}_{0}(\Omega)),\qquad\zeta\bm{U}_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];H^{2}_{0}(\Omega)),

where
\zeta=\zeta(\bm{y})=\zeta(r)
defined on
\Omega
. This implies
\zeta\bm{U}\in C([0,T];H^{3}_{0}(\Omega))
due to Lemma
A.9
. Hence, using Lemma
B.1
again, we obtain
r\mathscr{D}_{r}^{k}U\in C([0,T];L^{2}(I_{\flat}))
for
k=0,1,2,3
, which, along with Lemmas
A.4
–
A.5
,
implies

(U,\mathscr{D}_{r}U,\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U)\in C([0,T];L^{2}(I_{\flat}))\implies(U,\mathscr{D}_{r}U)\in C([0,T];C(\bar{I}_{\flat})).

Next, it follows similarly from
\eqref{bbbb}_{1}
and Lemmas
B.1
and
C.1
that

t\zeta\nabla_{\bm{y}}^{2}\bm{U}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];H^{2}_{0}(\Omega)),\qquad(t\zeta\nabla_{\bm{y}}^{2}\bm{U})_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}(\Omega)),

which, along with Lemma
A.9
, leads to
t\zeta\nabla_{\bm{y}}^{2}\bm{U}\in C([0,T];W^{1,4}(\Omega))
. This, together with Lemma
B.1
, implies

(2.80)

r^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U,\partial_{r}(\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U))\in C((0,T];L^{4}(I_{\flat})).

On the other hand, since, for any function
f=f(r)
,
|f|_{1}\leq C|r^{\frac{1}{2}}f|_{4}
in
I_{\flat}
due to the Hölder inequality, we can derive from (
2.80
) and Lemma
A.4
that

\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U\in C((0,T];W^{1,1}(I_{\flat}))\implies\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U\in C((0,T];C(\bar{I}_{\flat})),

which completes the proof of (
2.79
).

7.2. Regularity of
U
away from the origin.
Define
I_{\sharp}:=(\frac{1}{2},1)
. To obtain (
2.78
), it still remains to show

(2.81)

U\in C([0,T];C^{1}(\bar{I}_{\sharp})),\qquad(U_{rr},U_{t})\in C((0,T];C(\bar{I}_{\sharp})).

First, it follows from (
2.68
) that

(2.82)

(\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U,\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}U_{r},\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t})\in C([0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp})).

Next, we obtain from (
2.69
),
\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}>-\frac{1}{2}
, and Lemmas
A.5
and
C.1
that
\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(U_{rr},U_{rrr})\in H^{1}(I_{\sharp})
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
. Hence,
\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(U_{rr},U_{rrr})\in C(\bar{I}_{\sharp})
due to Lemma
A.4
, which, along with (
2.69
) again, leads to

\displaystyle\zeta_{\frac{1}{3}}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}(U_{rr},U_{rrr})

\displaystyle\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2})\cap L^{2}([0,T];H^{1}_{0}),

\displaystyle\zeta_{\frac{1}{3}}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}(U_{trr},U_{trrr})

\displaystyle\in L^{2}([0,T];H^{-1}).

Then it follows from the above and Lemma
A.9
that

(2.83)

\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}(U_{rr},U_{rrr})\in C([0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp})).

(
2.83
), combined with (
2.82
) and Lemma
A.5
, yields
U\in C([0,T];W^{2,1}(I_{\sharp}))
, which, along with Lemma
A.4
, leads to
\eqref{classical-ex}_{1}
.

It remains to prove
\eqref{classical-ex}_{2}
. To this end, we first obtain from (
2.68
) that

tU_{tt}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\cap L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}^{1}),\qquad r^{2}\rho_{0}(tU_{tt})_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];\mathcal{H}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta}}^{-1}),

This, together with (
2.69
) and Lemmas
A.4
and
A.10
, gives

(2.84)

t(U_{tr},U_{tt})\in C([0,T];L^{2}_{r^{2}\rho_{0}})\implies\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U_{tr},U_{tt})\in C((0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp})).

Now, following an argument similar to the proof of
\sqrt{t}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U_{t}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp}))

(this proof is omitted in Step 6 above; see Steps 2.1 and 3 in the proof of Lemma
3.6
below for details),
with the help of time-continuities (
2.82
)–(
2.84
) and the regularities of
(\bar{U},\bar{\eta})
in (
2.4
)–(
2.5
), we also have
\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U_{t}\in C((0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp}))
. Then from the chain rules, we obtain

(2.85)

\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}U_{trr}\in C((0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp})).

Finally, recalling (
2.72
), we similarly define

\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}^{*}=\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}^{*}(t,r):=U_{rrrr}+(\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}+2)\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}}U_{rrr}.

Clearly, based on (
2.83
)–(
2.85
), the chain rules and the regularity of
\bar{\eta}
in (
2.5
), we can follow a similar argument in Steps 2.2 and 3 of Lemma
3.6
(this argument is actually contained in Step 6 above, however, we omit it) and obtain
\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}\bar{\mathcal{T}}^{*}_{\mathrm{cross}}\in C((0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp}))
. Then, by Lemma
D.1
, for any
0<t,t_{0}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}(U_{rrrr}(t)-U_{rrrr}(t_{0}))\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}\big(\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}(t)-\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{\mathrm{cross}}(t_{0}),U_{rrr}(t)-U_{rrr}(t_{0})\big)\big|_{2}.

Passing to the limit
t\to t_{0}
, together with (
2.83
), yields

(2.86)

\rho_{0}^{\frac{(\gamma-1)(3-\varepsilon_{0})}{2}}U_{rrrr}\in C((0,T];L^{2}(I_{\sharp})).

Thus, it follows from from (
2.83
)–(
2.86
) and Lemma
A.5
that
(U_{rr},U_{t})\in C((0,T];W^{1,1}(I_{\sharp}))
, which, along with Lemma
A.4
, yields
\eqref{classical-ex}_{2}
. This completes the proof of (
2.81
), and hence the proof of (
2.78
).

Step 8. Derivation of the boundary condition.
The boundary condition of
U
in (
1.32
) can be proved by basically follow the argument used in Remark
1.3
of §
1
. First, the boundary condition
U|_{r=0}=0
follows directly from
\frac{U}{r}\in C([0,T];C(\bar{I}))
. Next, since
\eqref{lp}_{1}
holds pointwise in
(0,T]\times(0,1)
, we can divide
\eqref{lp}_{1}
by
\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}
to obtain

(2.87)

\bar{\varrho}U_{t}+AD_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma})+4\pi G\frac{\bar{\varrho}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}=(2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}\Big(\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big)-4a_{1}D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{\delta})\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}},

where
\bar{\varrho}=\frac{r^{2}\rho_{0}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}
. Then multiplying the above by
\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-\delta-1}
gives

(2.88)

\displaystyle\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})\Big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U+2a_{2}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-\delta}U_{t}+\frac{A\gamma}{\gamma-1}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1})+4\pi G\frac{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-\delta}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}

\displaystyle-(2a_{1}+a_{2})\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U+2D_{\bar{\eta}}\big(\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big).

Due to the facts that
1-r\sim\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\in C^{1}(\bar{I})
and

(2.89)

(\mathscr{D}_{r}U,\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}U,\mathscr{D}_{r}\bar{\eta},\mathscr{D}_{r}^{2}\bar{\eta})\in C((0,T];C(\bar{I})),\qquad(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})\in\big[\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{2}\big],

we see that the right-hand side of (
2.88
) belongs to
C((0,T]\times\bar{I})
and vanishes at the boundary
\{r=1\}
. Consequently, taking the limit as
r\to 1
in (
2.88
), we obtain from
(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{r=1}\neq 0
that

(2.90)

D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1})\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{r=1}=0\implies\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big|_{r=1}=0.

Step 9. Equation
\eqref{lp}_{1}
holds pointwise in
(0,T]\times\bar{I}
.
By Definition
2.1
, it remains to show that
U(t,r)
satisfies equation
\eqref{lp}_{1}
pointwise in
(0,T]\times\bar{I}
. However, to make the method in this step applicable to the well-posedness of the nonlinear problem (
1.22
), we consider a more general case here. More precisely, we show that
\eqref{lp}_{1}\times(\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r})^{-1}
, that is, (
2.87
) holds pointwise in
(0,T]\times\bar{I}
. Furthermore, based on the structure of (
2.87
), it suffices to show that the “most singular” term

\mathbb{S}:=D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{\delta})\underline{\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)}_{:=\mathbb{S}_{*}}\qquad\text{holds pointwise in $(0,T]\times\bar{I}$}.

A direct calculation gives

(2.91)

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}

\displaystyle=-\delta\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\delta-1}\Big(\frac{2r^{3}}{\bar{\eta}^{3}\bar{\eta}_{r}^{2}}\big(\frac{\bar{\eta}}{r}\big)_{r}+\frac{r^{2}\bar{\eta}_{rr}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}^{3}}\Big)\rho_{0}^{\delta}\mathbb{S}_{*}+\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}\Big(\frac{r^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}\Big)^{\delta}\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}}{\bar{\eta}_{r}}\rho_{0}^{\delta-(\gamma-1)}\mathbb{S}_{*}.

From (
2.89
), it follows that the first term in (
2.91
) belong to
C((0,T]\times\bar{I})
, hence holding pointwise in
(0,T]\times\bar{I}
.

To prove that the second term in (
2.91
) holds pointwise in
(0,T]\times\bar{I}
, we only need to check

(2.92)

\rho_{0}^{\delta-\gamma+1}\mathbb{S}_{*}|_{r=1}<\infty\qquad\text{on }(0,T].

Indeed, due to (
2.90
) and
(\mathscr{D}_{r}^{k}U,\mathscr{D}_{r}^{k}\bar{\eta})\in C((0,T];C(\bar{I}))
(
k=0,1,2
), we have

|\mathbb{S}_{*}|=\Big|\int_{r}^{1}(\mathbb{S}_{*})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}\Big|\leq C(T)(1-r)\qquad\text{for any $(t,r)\in(0,T]\times\bar{I}$}.

Hence, we obtain that,
|\rho_{0}^{\delta-\gamma+1}\mathbb{S}_{*}|\leq C(T)\rho_{0}^{\delta}
for all
(t,r)\in(0,T]\times I
, which gives (
2.92
).

This completes the proof of Lemma
2.1
.

3.
Uniform Estimates to the Linearized Problem

In this section, based on Lemma
2.1
, we will establish the uniform estimates
for the classical solution
U
of problem (
2.1
).
In §
3
–§
4
,
C\in(1,\infty)
denotes a generic constant depending only on
(a_{1},a_{2},A,\delta,\gamma,G)
, and
C(l_{1},\cdots\!,l_{k})\in(1,\infty)
a generic constant depending on
C

and the additional parameters
(l_{1},\cdots\!,l_{k})
, which may be different at each occurrence. Moreover, for simplicity, we denote
\mathfrak{p}(\cdot)
a generic polynomial function, taking the form:

\mathfrak{p}(s)=\sum_{j=1}^{k}s^{j}\qquad\text{with some $k\in\mathbb{N}^{*}$}.

Let
(\rho_{0},u_{0})
be given initial data satisfying the hypothesis of Lemma
2.1
.
Assume that
there exists a constant
c_{0}>1
such that

(3.1)

1+K_{1}+K_{2}+\|\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\|_{H^{3}(\Omega)}+\mathcal{E}(0,U)\leq c_{0}.

Then fix
T>0
, and assume that there exist some constants
T^{*}\in(0,T]
and
c_{1}
such that
1<c_{0}\leq c_{1}
and, for all
t\in[0,T^{*}]
,

(3.2)

\bar{\mathcal{E}}(t,\bar{U})+t\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})+\int_{0}^{t}\bar{\mathcal{D}}(s,\bar{U})\,\mathrm{d}s\leq c_{1},\qquad|\bar{\eta}_{r}(t)-1|_{\infty}+\Big|\frac{\bar{\eta}(t)}{r}-1\Big|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Here,
(c_{1},T^{*})
will be determined later, which depend only on
(c_{0},\varepsilon_{0},a_{1},a_{2},A,\delta,\gamma,G,T)
.

3.1.
Preliminary

First, we have the following estimates associated with
\bar{U}
.

Lemma 3.1
.

For any
t\in[0,T^{*}]
,

\big|(\bar{U},\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U})\big|_{\infty}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1}),\qquad\big|(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U},\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{\infty}\leq C(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}),

and, for any
a\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big|_{4}\leq C(a)\mathfrak{p}(c_{1}),\qquad\big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{4}\leq C(a)(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U},\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{\infty}\leq C(a)\mathfrak{p}(c_{1}),\qquad\big|\chi_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{\infty}\leq C(a)(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Proof.

The proof can be directly derived by (
3.2
) and Lemmas
A.4
–
A.5
. For example, it follows that, for all
t\in[0,T^{*}]
and
a\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C\big|(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U},\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}\bar{U})\big|_{1}\leq C\sum_{j=2}^{4}\Big(\big|\chi r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{j}\bar{U}\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{j}\bar{U}\big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle\big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{5}{4}}(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t},\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{2}\leq C(a)\big(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big),

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3(\gamma-1)}{2}}(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{2}\leq C(a)\big(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big).

The rest of this lemma can be proved analogously, we omit the details here for brevity.
∎

Next, to further simplify the calculations, we define the quantities:

(3.3)

\bar{\Lambda}:=D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}=D_{\bar{\eta}}(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\bar{\mathscr{J}}^{1-\gamma}),\qquad\bar{\mathscr{J}}:=\frac{\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}}{r^{2}},\qquad\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}:=\frac{4\pi G}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}.

Then we obtain some useful estimates for
(\bar{\Lambda},\bar{\mathscr{J}},\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi})
.

Lemma 3.2
.

For any
0\leq t\leq T_{1}=\min\{T^{*},\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})^{-1}\}
,
a\in(0,1)
, and
\sigma>0
,

\displaystyle\big|\big(\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi},\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi},\chi_{a}^{\sharp}(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi})^{2}\big)\big|_{\infty}\leq\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),\qquad\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda}\big|_{4}+\big|\zeta_{a}r(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda},\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{\Lambda})\big|_{2}\leq C(a)\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),

\displaystyle|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),\qquad|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\mathscr{J}}|_{\infty}+|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{\Lambda}\big|_{2}\leq C(a)\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(-\frac{1}{2}+\sigma)}\bar{\Lambda}\big|_{2}\leq C(a,\sigma)\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),\qquad|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\Lambda}_{t}|_{4}+|\chi_{a}^{\sharp}\Lambda_{t}|_{\infty}\leq C(a)\mathfrak{p}(c_{1}).

Proof.

First, it follows from (
3.2
) that

\displaystyle\big|\,\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}\,\big|_{\infty}=\Big|\frac{4\pi G}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\int_{0}^{r}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}\Big|_{\infty}\leq C\Big|\frac{1}{r^{2}}\int_{0}^{1}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}\Big|_{\infty}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),

\displaystyle|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}|_{\infty}\leq C\Big|\frac{1}{r^{3}}\int_{0}^{1}\hat{r}^{2}\rho_{0}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{r}\Big|_{\infty}+C\big|\frac{r^{2}\rho_{0}}{r^{2}}\big|_{\infty}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Next, by (
2.3
) and (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), we see that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T^{*}]\times\bar{I}
,

(3.4)

\bar{\mathscr{J}}_{t}=\bar{\mathscr{J}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+\frac{2\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big),\qquad C^{-1}\leq\bar{\mathscr{J}}(t,r)\leq C.

Then we can obtain from (
3.4
) and Lemma
3.1
that

\displaystyle|D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\mathscr{J}}|

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{Ct\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})}\int_{0}^{t}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}|\,\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle|D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{\mathscr{J}}|

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{2Ct\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}|\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{2}+e^{Ct\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})}\int_{0}^{t}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}\bar{U}|\,\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle|D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}\bar{\mathscr{J}}|

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{3Ct\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}|\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{3}+Ce^{2Ct\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}|\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}\bar{U}|\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)

\displaystyle+Ce^{Ct\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})}\int_{0}^{t}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{4}\bar{U}|\,\mathrm{d}s.

On the other hand, by the chain rules, we have

(3.5)

\displaystyle D_{\bar{\eta}}^{k}\bar{\Lambda}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=0}^{k+1}C_{k,j}(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{k+1-j}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})D_{\bar{\eta}}^{j}(\bar{\mathscr{J}}^{1-\gamma})\qquad\text{for $k=0,1,2$},

\displaystyle\bar{\Lambda}_{t}

\displaystyle=(1-\gamma)\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}D_{\bar{\eta}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+\frac{2\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)-\bar{\Lambda}\Big(\gamma D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2(\gamma-1)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big),

where
C_{k,j}
are some constants depend only on
(k,j)
.

Therefore, for all
0\leq t\leq T_{1}:=\min\{T^{*},\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})^{-1}\}
, combining (
3.1
)–(
3.5
), together with (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), (
3.4
), Lemmas
3.1
and
A.4
–
A.5
, and the Hölder and Minkowski inequalities, we can recursively obtain the desired estimates of this lemma.
∎

3.2.
Uniform Estimates for the Velocity

The proof is divided into the following lemmas.

Lemma 3.3
.

For all
0\leq t\leq T_{2}=\min\{T_{1},\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})^{-1},\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})^{-1}\}
.

\big|r(\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U,\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U,\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t})(t)\big|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

Step 1.
Multiplying
\eqref{lp}_{1}
by
U
and integrating the resulting equation over
I
, along with (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), Lemma
3.2
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, yields that

(3.6)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+J_{1}

\displaystyle=A\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+2\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r-\int_{0}^{1}r^{2}\rho_{0}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}U\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}}{4}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}^{2}.

Here
J_{1}
is defined in the same way as
L_{1}
in (
2.20
), except with
(X,Y)=\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U
, and similarly to (
2.21
), we can find a constant
c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}>0
such that

(3.7)

J_{1}\geq c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}^{2}.

Then, we obtain from (
3.6
) and the Grönwall inequality that

(3.8)

\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq Ce^{C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})t}\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\qquad\text{for }\ t\in[0,T_{2}].

Step 2.

Multiplying
\eqref{lp}_{1}
by
U_{t}
and integrating the resulting equation over
I
, we obtain from (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), Lemma
3.1
, and the Young inequality that, for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}J_{1}+\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=A\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+\frac{2U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r-\int_{0}^{t}r^{2}\rho_{0}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1-\delta}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}})\Big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U^{2}+4a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}U\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\frac{U^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(\big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U^{2}+2a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}U\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+\big(2a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}U\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\frac{U^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\big)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})+\varepsilon\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+C\big|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big|_{\infty}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}^{2}

(3.9)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})\big(\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big)+\varepsilon\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Step 3.

Next, multiplying (
2.44
) by
U_{t}
and integrating the resulting equation over
I
implies that

(3.10)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+J_{2}

\displaystyle=\underline{\int_{0}^{1}(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(g^{(1)}\frac{2U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}+h^{(1)}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=J_{3}}.

Here
J_{2}
is defined in the same way as
L_{1}
in (
2.20
), except with
(X,Y)=\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}
, and similarly to (
2.21
), we can find a constant
c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}>0
such that

(3.11)

J_{2}\geq c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

For
J_{3}
, recalling
(g^{(1)},h^{(1)})
in (
2.45
), we derive from (
3.1
)–(
3.2
) and Lemma
3.1
that

(3.12)

\displaystyle|(g^{(1)},h^{(1)})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big|_{\infty}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(|\bar{\varrho}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}+|\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}|_{\infty}\big)|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})\big(\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}+\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big).

Then it follows from (
3.2
), (
3.12
), and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(3.13)

\displaystyle J_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})\big(\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big)+\frac{c_{a_{1},a_{2}}^{*}}{8}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Then, substituting (
3.11
)–(
3.13
) into (
3.10
) yields that

(3.14)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+C\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})\big(\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big).

Finally, combing with (
3.14
) and (
3.9
), and then taking
\varepsilon
sufficiently small in (
3.9
), together with (
3.2
), (
3.7
), and the Grönwall inequality, yields that, for all
0\leq t\leq T_{2}
,

(3.15)

\displaystyle\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})t}(\bar{\mathcal{E}}(0,U)+\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}))\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 3.4
.

For all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t},\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}\sqrt{t}U_{tt}\big)(t)\big|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(U_{tt},\sqrt{s}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\big)\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

Step 1.
First, it follows from (
2.56
) and (
3.3
) that

(3.16)

\displaystyle D_{\bar{\eta}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}\frac{\bar{\Lambda}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)+\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}U_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{A}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{\gamma}{\gamma-1}\bar{\Lambda}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}+\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}.

Then, due to the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
, (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), and Lemmas
A.11
and
3.2
–
3.3
, we see that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

(3.17)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta r\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}(\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}})\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C\Big|\zeta rD_{\bar{\eta}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}|_{\infty}|\zeta rU_{t}|_{2}+|\zeta r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U|_{2}|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}+(|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}+|\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}|_{\infty})|\zeta r\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}|_{2}\big)\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),

which, along with Lemma
A.5
, also leads to

(3.18)

\big|\zeta r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{4}\leq\big|r^{\frac{5}{4}}\big(\zeta\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U,\zeta_{r}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U,\zeta\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\big)\big|_{2}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Next, using (
3.8
), (
3.15
), and the similar argument as (
2.57
)–(
2.59
) in the proof of Lemma
2.4
, we obtain that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

(3.19)

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta-(\gamma-1)}{2}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\big|_{\infty}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Of course, based on (
3.15
), we also derive from (
3.12
) that, for all
t\in[0,T_{1}]
,

(3.20)

|(g^{(1)},h^{(1)})|_{2}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1}).

Finally, recall
((g^{(1)})_{t},(h^{(1)})_{t})
in (
2.65
). It then follows from (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), (
3.18
)–(
3.19
), Lemmas
3.1
,
3.3
, and
A.5
, and the Hölder inequality that

(3.21)

\displaystyle\ \begin{aligned} \big|\chi((g^{(1)})_{t},(h^{(1)})_{t})\big|_{2}&\leq\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big((|\chi r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U|_{4}+1)|\chi r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}|_{4}+|\chi r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}|_{2}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})(|\chi r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U|_{2}+1)|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|_{\infty}^{2}\\
&\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big),\end{aligned}

(3.22)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}((g^{(1)})_{t},(h^{(1)})_{t})\big|_{2}&\leq C(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}+1)|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|_{\infty}^{2}+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t})\big|_{2}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{2}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta-(\gamma-1)}{2}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}-\frac{3}{4}(\gamma-1)}U\big|_{4}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{4}(\gamma-1)}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t}\big|_{4}\\
&\leq C(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big|_{2}+1)|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|_{\infty}^{2}+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t})\big|_{2}|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3(\gamma-1)}{2}}(\bar{U}_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{2}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta-(\gamma-1)}{2}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U)\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}(\gamma-1)}(\bar{U}_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big).\end{aligned}

Step 2.
Now, multiplying (
2.44
) by
U_{tt}
and integrating over
I
, we have

(3.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}J_{2}+\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}=\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}J_{4}-J_{5},

with

\displaystyle J_{4}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{1}(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(g^{(1)}\frac{2U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}+h^{(1)}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r,

\displaystyle J_{5}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}^{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(2a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\frac{U_{t}^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{1}(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\big(g^{(1)}\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}-(g^{(1)})_{t}\big)\frac{2U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}+(h^{(1)}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}-(h^{(1)})_{t})D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big)\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\frac{1-\delta}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}})\Big((2a_{1}+a_{2})D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}^{2}+4a_{2}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\frac{U_{t}^{2}}{\bar{\eta}^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

For
J_{2}
, we have obtained in (
3.11
) that

J_{2}\geq c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2},

and
J_{4}
can be handled by

(3.24)

|J_{4}|\leq C\varepsilon^{-1}\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})+\varepsilon|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\qquad\text{for all $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$}.

For
J_{5}
, it follows from (
3.20
)–(
3.22
), Lemma
3.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(3.25)

\displaystyle J_{5}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\big|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big|_{\infty}|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\big|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big|_{\infty}|(g^{(1)},h^{(1)})|_{2}\!+|((g^{(1)})_{t},(h^{(1)})_{t})|_{2}\big)|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})\big(1+|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}.

Thus, substituting (
3.24
)–(
3.25
) into (
3.23
) with
\varepsilon
sufficiently small, we can deduce from the Grönwall inequality that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\bar{\mathcal{D}}(s,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

To further simplified the above inequality, define

\mathcal{Y}(t)=C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\bar{\mathcal{D}}(s,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

Then
\mathcal{F}(t)\leq\mathcal{Y}(t)
and

\mathcal{Y}^{\prime}(t)=C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{Y}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Clearly, this, together with (
3.2
), implies that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

\mathcal{Y}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\leq\mathcal{Y}^{\frac{1}{2}}(0)+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\int_{0}^{t}\bar{\mathcal{D}}(s,\bar{U})^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})(c_{1}t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}),

which yields that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

(3.26)

|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Step 3.
Since we have shown that
U
satisfies (
2.37
) with
(w,g,h)
replaced by
(U_{t},g^{(1)},h^{(1)})
in Step 4 of §
2.3
, we have

(3.27)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}+J_{6}=J_{7}+J_{8},

where
J_{6}
is defined in the same way as
L_{1}
in (
2.20
), except with
(X,Y)=\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}
, and

(3.28)

\displaystyle J_{7}

\displaystyle:=(2a_{1}+a_{2})\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(-(\delta+1)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+(2-2\delta)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+4(a_{1}+a_{2})\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big((1-\delta)D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}-2\delta\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\frac{U_{tt}}{\bar{\eta}}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+2a_{2}\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\eta}^{2}\bar{\eta}_{r}\bar{\varrho}^{\delta}\Big(-\delta D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+(1-2\delta)\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\Big(\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}+D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\frac{U_{tt}}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r,

\displaystyle J_{8}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{1}(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\big(g^{(1)}_{t}-g^{(1)}\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\frac{2U_{tt}}{\bar{\eta}}+\big(h^{(1)}_{t}-h^{(1)}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big)D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

For
J_{6}
, similarly to (
2.21
), we can find a constant
c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}>0
such that

(3.29)

J_{6}\geq c^{*}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

For
J_{7}
–
J_{8}
, it follows from (
3.20
)–(
3.22
), (
3.26
), Lemma
3.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(3.30)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} J_{7}&\leq C\big|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big|_{\infty}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\frac{c_{a_{1},a_{2}}^{*}}{100}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

(3.31)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} J_{8}&\leq C\big(\big|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}\big|_{\infty}|(g^{(1)},h^{(1)})|_{2}\!+|((g^{(1)})_{t},(h^{(1)})_{t})|_{2}\big)\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U}))+\frac{c_{a_{1},a_{2}}^{*}}{100}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.\end{aligned}

Thus, plugging (
3.29
)–(
3.31
) into (
3.27
), we obtain from (
3.2
) that

(3.32)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(\mathfrak{p}(c_{1})+\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,\bar{U})).

Multiplying (
3.32
) by
t
and integrating over
[\tau,t]
with
\tau\in(0,t)
, along with (
3.2
) and (
3.26
), gives

(3.33)

t\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{t}s\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\eta}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq\tau\big|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(\tau)\big|_{2}^{2}+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{1}).

Thanks to (
3.26
) and Lemma
A.7
, we can find a sequence
\{\tau_{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
such that
\tau_{k}\to 0
and
\tau_{k}|(r^{2}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(\tau_{k})|_{2}\to 0
as
k\to\infty
. Hence, taking
\tau=\tau_{k}
in (
3.33
) and then letting
k\to\infty
, we finally obtain the desired estimate.
∎

Lemma 3.5
.

For any
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

(3.34)

\bar{\mathcal{E}}(t,U)+\big|(U,\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U\big)(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

Step 1. Boundedness of
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)
.
It only remains to establish the third-order elliptic estimate for
U
near the origin. First, it follows from (
2.56
) that

(3.35)

\displaystyle D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\big(\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+\frac{1-\delta}{\gamma-1}\Lambda\bar{\varrho}^{2-\gamma-\delta}U_{t}\big)-\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}\frac{\bar{\Lambda}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}\big(\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}\big(\frac{D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}+\frac{\bar{\Lambda}^{2}}{\bar{\varrho}^{2(\gamma-1)}}\big)\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{A}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{\gamma}{\gamma-1}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}+\frac{1-\delta}{\gamma-1}\bar{\Lambda}^{2}\bar{\varrho}^{2-\delta-\gamma}\big)+\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}D_{\bar{\eta}}(\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}).

Then it follows from the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
, (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), (
3.16
)–(
3.18
), (
3.35
), and Lemmas
3.2
–
3.3
,
A.5
, and
A.11
that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

(3.36)

\displaystyle\big|\zeta r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big|\zeta rD_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{2}+\Big|\zeta r\frac{1}{\bar{\eta}}D_{\bar{\eta}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(\big|\zeta r\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda}\big|_{4}|\zeta r^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U|_{4}\big)

\displaystyle+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(\big|\zeta r(\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U,\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda})\big|_{2}+|\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}|_{\infty}+1\big)\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Step 2. Boundedness of
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)
.
It only remains to establish the second- and third-order elliptic estimates for
U
away from the origin.

2.1.
First, rewrite (
3.16
) as

(3.37)

\displaystyle D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U

\displaystyle=-2D_{\bar{\eta}}(\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}})-\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}\frac{\bar{\Lambda}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}\Big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\Big)+\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}U_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{A}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{\gamma}{\gamma-1}\bar{\Lambda}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}+\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}.

Then it follows from the above, (
3.1
)–(
3.2
), (
3.19
), and Lemmas
3.2
–
3.3
that, for all
\varepsilon>0
,

(3.38)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}+\varepsilon}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta}{2}+\varepsilon}(\rho_{0}^{1-\delta}U_{t},U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-\frac{\gamma-1}{2}+\varepsilon}\bar{\Lambda}\big|_{2}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\delta-(\gamma-1)}{2}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\delta}{2}+\varepsilon}\big|_{2}(|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}+|\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}|_{\infty})\leq C(\varepsilon)\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Clearly, since
(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})(\gamma-1)>\frac{\delta}{2}
, the above also leads to

(3.39)

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\big|_{2}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

2.2.

Next, Indeed, using an argument similar to (
2.57
)–(
2.59
), we can obtain that, for all
\iota<\delta+\frac{\gamma-1}{2}
and
\sigma>0
, and for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

(3.40)

\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\iota-(\gamma-1)+\sigma}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(\sigma,\iota)\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\iota}\big(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,\rho_{0}^{1-\delta}U_{t}\big)\big|_{2}\big).

Then, due to the facts that

\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{1}{100},\qquad\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)(\gamma-1)>\frac{1}{2},

we can choose fixed
(\iota,\sigma)
in (
3.40
) such that

\iota+\sigma=\big(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)(\gamma-1),\qquad\iota<\delta+\frac{\gamma-1}{2},\qquad 0<\sigma<\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)(\gamma-1)-\frac{1}{2}.

Hence, it follows from the above, (
3.39
), and Lemmas
3.3
–
3.4
and
A.5
that

(3.41)

\displaystyle\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})(\gamma-1)}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})-\sigma}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,\rho_{0}^{1-\delta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})-\sigma}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Finally, based on (
3.37
), using an argument similar to (
2.57
)–(
2.59
), together with the fact
(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})>\frac{1}{2}
, (
3.41
), and Lemmas
3.2
–
3.4
and
A.5
, yields that, for all
0\leq t\leq T_{2}
,

(3.42)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}+|(\bar{\Lambda},\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi})|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle+C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})(\gamma-1)}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{2}|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0})\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t})\big|_{2}+1\big)\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

2.3.
Based on (
3.35
), we obtain from (
3.39
)–(
3.42
) and Lemmas
3.2
–
3.4
and
A.5
, that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

(3.43)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{3}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t})\big|_{2}+C\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}(D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U,D_{\bar{\eta}}U,U)\big|_{2}|\Lambda|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+C\big(|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}+|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})(\gamma-1)}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\big(|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}+|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}^{2}+|\bar{\Lambda}|_{\infty}|(\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi},D_{\bar{\eta}}(\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}))|_{\infty}\big)\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Step 3.
Finally, it follows from (
3.17
), (
3.36
), (
3.39
), (
3.43
), and Lemmas
3.3
–
3.4
and
A.4
–
A.5
that, for all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

\displaystyle\big|(U,\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U)\big|_{\infty}\leq C\big|(U,\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}U,\mathscr{D}_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U)\big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{j=0}^{3}\big(\big|\chi rD_{\bar{\eta}}^{j}U\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\gamma-1)(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{j}U\big|_{2}\big)+C\sum_{j=0}^{2}\Big|\zeta rD_{\bar{\eta}}^{j}(\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}})\Big|_{2}\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 3.6
.

For all
t\in[0,T_{2}]
,

t\bar{\mathcal{D}}(t,U)+\int_{0}^{t}\bar{\mathcal{D}}(s,U)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\mathfrak{p}(c_{0}).

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

Step 1.
L^{1}(0,T)
-boundedness of
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)
.

1.1.

First, applying
\partial_{t}
to (
3.16
) yields that

(3.44)

D_{\bar{\eta}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+\frac{2U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)=\sum_{i=9}^{11}J_{i},

where

\displaystyle J_{9}

\displaystyle:=D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}\bar{U}D_{\bar{\eta}}U+2D_{\bar{\eta}}(\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}})\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}+2D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}D_{\bar{\eta}}^{2}U+2\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)D_{\bar{\eta}}(\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}),

\displaystyle J_{10}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}\big(U_{tt}-(1-\delta)\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)U_{t}\big)-\frac{\delta}{\gamma-1}\frac{\bar{\Lambda}_{t}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}\big)

(3.45)

\displaystyle\quad-\delta\frac{\bar{\Lambda}}{\bar{\varrho}^{\gamma-1}}\Big(\big(\frac{\gamma-2}{\gamma-1}D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)D_{\bar{\eta}}U

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+\frac{2\gamma-3}{\gamma-1}\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\frac{U}{\bar{\eta}}+\frac{1}{\gamma-1}\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}U_{t}+\frac{2a_{2}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle J_{11}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\bar{\varrho}^{1-\delta}}{2a_{1}+a_{2}}\Big(\frac{A\gamma}{\gamma-1}\big(\bar{\Lambda}_{t}-(1-\delta)\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\big)-\overline{D_{\eta}\Phi}\big((1-\delta)\big(D_{\bar{\eta}}\bar{U}+2\frac{\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)+\frac{2\bar{U}}{\bar{\eta}}\big)\Big).

Then, by the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\sim 1-r
, (
3.1
), (
3.17
)–(
3.18
), Lemmas
