\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{c+1}\Big(\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}d^{c+1}d_{j}^{-2\vartheta}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}d^{c+1}d_{j}^{-2\vartheta}ff_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\vartheta}{c+1}\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}d^{c}d_{j}^{-2\vartheta}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r,

which implies

(D.10)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}d^{c}d_{j}^{-2\vartheta}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{b}\Big(\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}d^{c+1}d_{j}^{-2\vartheta}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}d^{c+1}d_{j}^{-2\vartheta}ff_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}d^{\frac{c+1}{2}}d_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}d^{\frac{c+1}{2}}d_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\big|_{2}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}d^{\frac{c+1}{2}}d_{j}^{-\vartheta}f_{r}\big|_{2}\big).

This completes the proof of (
D.9
).

Now, we continue to prove (
D.4
). It follows from (
D.3
) and
0\leq\varphi/\varphi_{j}\leq 1
that

(D.11)

Q_{j}:=\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}(\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}f_{r}+c\varphi^{\frac{c-1}{2}}\varphi_{r}f)\in L^{2}\qquad\text{for any $j\in\mathbb{N}^{*}$}.

Using a density argument similar to that in Step 2, we can show that the following integral equality still holds,
i.e.
, for all
f\in H^{1}_{\varphi^{c+1}}(\frac{1}{2},1)
and
j\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
,

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\varphi^{c}\varphi_{j}^{-2\vartheta}\varphi_{r}ff_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\big(2\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\varphi_{r}+(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\varphi_{rr}\big)\varphi^{c}\varphi_{j}^{-2\vartheta}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\big(c\varphi^{c-1}\varphi_{j}^{-2\vartheta}-2\vartheta\varphi^{c}\varphi_{j}^{-2\vartheta-1}\big)(\varphi_{r})^{2}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r.

Hence, based on the above equality and the calculation similar to (
D.5
), we deduce from (
D.11
) and the Young inequality that

\displaystyle\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f_{r}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=|Q_{j}|_{2}^{2}-c^{2}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c-1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}\varphi_{r}f\big|_{2}^{2}-2c\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\varphi^{c}\varphi_{j}^{-2\vartheta}\varphi_{r}ff_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=|Q_{j}|_{2}^{2}+c\int_{0}^{1}\big(2\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\varphi_{r}+(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\varphi_{rr}\big)\varphi^{c}\varphi_{j}^{-2\vartheta}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\underline{-2\vartheta c\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\varphi^{c-1}\varphi_{j}^{-2\vartheta-1}(\varphi_{r})^{2}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r}_{\,\leq 0}

\displaystyle\leq|Q_{j}|_{2}^{2}+C\big(|(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\varphi^{-1}\varphi_{r}|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\big|_{2}^{2}+|\chi^{\sharp}\varphi_{rr}|_{\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\big|_{2}^{2}\big),

which, along with (
D.1
) and (
D.9
), gives

\displaystyle\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(|Q_{j}|_{2}^{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\big|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}(\varphi^{b}f_{r}+c\varphi^{b-1}\varphi_{r}f)\big|_{2}+|\chi^{\sharp}\varphi^{b}f|_{2}\big).

Since
C
is independent of
j
, we can extract a subsequence (still denoted by
j
) such that

(D.12)

\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\to g\qquad\text{weakly in }L^{2}\quad\text{as }j\to\infty,

for some limit function
g\in L^{2}
, and

|g|_{2}\leq\liminf_{j\to\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\big|_{2}\leq C\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}(\varphi^{b}f_{r}+c\varphi^{b-1}\varphi_{r}f)\big|_{2}+|\chi^{\sharp}\varphi^{b}f|_{2}\big).

Note that
f_{r}\in L^{1}_{\mathrm{loc}}
, the Lebesgue dominated convergence theorem also gives

(D.13)

\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{\frac{c+1}{2}}\varphi_{j}^{-\vartheta}f\to\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{b}f_{r}\qquad\text{in }L^{1}_{\mathrm{loc}}\ \ \text{as }j\to\infty.

Hence, by (
D.12
)–(
D.13
) and the uniqueness of the limits, we have
g=\zeta^{\sharp}\varphi^{b}f_{r}
.

This completes the proof of Lemma
D.1
.
∎
